Requests model links self, nil, false; self, "xuid", true; self, "clientNum", true; self, "clientNum", true; self, "rank", true; self, "rankIcon", true; self, nil, false. Owns 9 nested callable scopes, each with its separate delivery/invocation contract. Registers LUI.OverrideFunction_CallOriginalSecond( self, "close", self.__onClose ). Runs PostLoadFunc( self, f1_arg1, f1_arg0 ) only under its exact guard and at its original source position. - `hmh07-lua-follow3:197` (original lines not specified): Creates CoD.CommonTabBarBacking using LUI.UIElement.new( f1_arg2, f1_arg3, f1_arg4, f1_arg5, f1_arg6, f1_arg7, f1_arg8, f1_arg9 ). Initializes and attaches children in source order: TabBackingBlur, TabBackingTint, NoiseTiledBacking, TabBottomLine. For each of the four created UIImage references, an independent IsPC call decides whether to delegate SizeToWidthOfScreen with that reference and controller. - `hmh07-lua:231` (original lines not specified): Supplied caller anchor is the Director factory definition itself, so external invocation/HMH menu navigation is unproven. Conditional constructor creates menu, setups default-focus callbacks/any-controller/offline-screen/build-info/companion-app/contracts model, then builds theater/custom/training/arena/public/private/pregame/select and shared/PC-looking children. Direct construction is not authority to reconstruct every other-mode child. Child menu_loaded wrappers use local/super menuLoaded, conditional non-PC safe-area sizing, then dispatch only if unhandled. State predicates: HideForGameStart from LobbyRoot hideMenusForGameStart1; ShowSelectKorea15Plus requires PC+Zombies+opaque Korea eligibility; other states delegate exact DirectorUtility ShowDirectorSelect/Pregame/Private/Custom/Arena/ArenaMatchmaking/Theater/Public/Training. lobbyNav/showSelect/hide flags reevaluate state; network/client/input/xuid/time/spinner/stage/session changes refresh button state. unused_gamepad_button invokes LobbyAddLocalClient(event.controller or owner) then dispatches children. Non-PC Start opens StartMenu_Main; non-PC social requires online/nonLAN/nonguest/allowed/not-demo-disabled/not-training, while PC select Triangle activates featured. Square branches offline-demo LAN hold behavior or nonPC select featured activation. Escape/mouse-keyboard and Circle/gamepad have separate ordered chains: hide preview, clear selected client, cancel/leave applicable custom/arena/theater/public states subject to spinner/timer/host/eligibility, or PC select quit. Duplicate preview branches are preserved and may be shadowed by earlier identical predicates; no guessed simplification. Back opens AAR only when CanShowAAR and refreshes prompt. RTrigger gestures requires dvar lobby_gestures_enabled plus opaque hash_61E20A632CB63092; then alias/preserve-button/open selector. setState CallOriginalFirst hook conditionally opens AAR/refreshes contract/client updates or initializes training; external predicates keep non-HMH internals conditional. Close CallOriginalFirst hook clears saved state and OnDirectorClose; class __onClose registered via CallOriginalSecond outside selected body remains dependency. lobby room/isMatchmaking callbacks refresh room; hideMenusForGameStart1 plays hide clip+loses focus/hides cursor,0 plays default/unhides cursor. menu_loaded then optional restore/postload; PC branch additionally key-color/friends/Korea/navigation/ZM-availability hooks, console branch DirectorUtility.PostLoad only. Exact default-focus registrations occur at end, then self returned. No live lobby/account action was executed; backend/model/enum/native dispatch, actual visibility and clip/close bodies remain open.

- `hmh07-lua:184` (original lines not specified): ui/main.lua1132..1165 builds common Hud unless existing/excluded gametype, then chooses WZ suffix for CoD.isWarzone OR gametype warzone (Campaign and CODFU/DOA overrides retained), and only when no T7HudMenuGameMode invokes Hud_WZ through dynamic factory unless non-WZ codcaster special case applies. Original effective flags/build remain conditional. Constructor initializes aarVisible/aarViewed only if unset, disabling automatic buttons only on PC, creates safe-area/ready/damage/chat/objective/results/scoreboard/hint/wheel/PC widgets. ReadyEvents scriptNotify dispatches hero_weapon_received/killstreak_received/levelup helpers; objective ShowNotification defaults are AlwaysFalse. MPHintText nonempty predicate and nonnil text link make this shared MP-named child usable on WZ. Sprays Visible requires held button plus no prematch-hide/last-stand/inventory/multi-pickup/remote/vehicle/demo/dead/round-end/scoreboard/spectator exclusions; Callouts similarly requires pressed+enabled and no theater/prematch/inventory/last-stand/dead/round-end/scoreboard/spectator exclusion. All corresponding model/input links reevaluate state, never produce gameplay input by themselves. Self states: HideAllButScoreboard=stage_ended; SpeedBoost=gadget_speed_burst plus gadget2 inuse; HideForCodCaster=caster with profile playerhud1. Circle/back hides scoreboard only through predicates and blocks the game event. Up opens quick inventory only with HUD weapon visibility, allowed inventory, no ended/demo/dead/scoreboard/spectating/UI/laststand/vehicle-hide/disable-setting; Down closes with its separate narrower gate. Weapon-next/prev/primary/secondary require Warzone, full inventory open and no demo. LB/RB/triggers and Cross prioritize health/equipment cycle actions when quick inventory open/profile cycle-items1/button-mapping agrees; they block the game event. Cross then has PC-gamepad deathcam fallback. PC-only spectate/deathcam/AAR/report branches retain exact additional conditions; if IsPC=false they do not execute, without excluding unconditional PC-looking child construction. CanUseQuickInventory notification closes quick inventory only if now disallowed and already open; character_unlock_update dispatches UpdateUnlockQuestState. menu_loaded processed, linked __onClose hook registered, captured f0_local0 postload invoked if present. PC final setup adds region checks/sizing; console final setup disables keyboard navigation and adds WZ armblade reticles; both setup grenade warnings/custom focus/cursor rules. Return self. Full exact callbacks/labels/branch ordering are in trace; unselected class clips/reset/close and external helper bodies are dependencies only, with no claim of original event delivery, network menu response or real execution.

- `hmh07-lua-follow4:123` (original lines 473-475, 473-475): Stores a ListSetup datasource under the supplied name and returns that name. The owned provider, only when invoked, builds image model rows from each supplied item whose chosen field exists, using current session mode and item-image requests. Passing the provider is distinct from executing it. Returns 0 offline. Online, scans all balance items for matching name, keeps the last match, subtracts a positive supplied adjustment and clamps below zero. It reads the AARPreMatch baseline when that field exists and returns the nonnegative difference. Recovered f59_local1..4 assignments lack local declarations here; no lexical repair is made. Returns without changes when Black Market is disabled. Otherwise loops the selected stream tier items, separates signature-weapon/outfit/gesture properties, rewrites weaponRef/rarity/unlocked/isLootCase, clears moved fields and appends models/properties rows unless the optional name filter rejects them. The local bribe-name predicate is defined then called immediately for each item; it is not a deferred service callback. The optional rarity dvar overrides the computed rarity. The fake-tier path returns currentTier minus three and 200 when the needed model exists. Otherwise uses AARPreMatch tiers/credits, replacing tiers with currentSeasonTiers when present; absent baseline data delegates to GetLootStreamData. Only two values are returned. Fake-tier mode returns currentTier, 800, lootTierMax and 1 when the current-tier model exists. Normal data returns currentTier/currentLootXP/lootTierMax and redeemed skips or 0. A missing progress/currentTier path returns four zeroes. Other child lookups in a present path are not individually guarded. Returns false outside public online play or with Black Market disabled. Otherwise the forced AAR overlay or LootCaseRewardUp returns true; the remaining path compares prematch tier against current tier. It does not grant rewards.

- `hmh07-lua-follow4:263` (original lines 2248-2257): cacRoot requests the hashed native root directly; otherwise chooses three-argument storagegetbuffer when arg2 is nonnil or the two-argument version. Returns the requested root/buffer. Weapon0 calls ClearDisplay then returns. Otherwise requests paintjob setup and a CustomClass_update local-client notify carrying the shown slot, converted weapon, empty attachments, camera and options fields. No network/persistence result is proved. Sends CustomClass_remove plus adjusted local-client number with an empty payload; no local cleanup completion is asserted. Returns editorMode != none from GetEmblemEditorProperties. Ends native edits; chooses paintshop/emblem customization and model notify; clears four per-controller selection fields; sets editor/color none and clipboard EMPTY; requests profile commit, optional paintjob invalidation and grid-size0. No rollback or commit success guard is added. Only uploadProfile==true clears the flag first and requests execnow uploadprofile. No retry/success notification or server implementation is supplied. For a supplied name matching an entitlement record and storage type, returns true at the first owned entitlement, else false for that matching record. No matching record or absent name defaults true. Both numeric loop headers are explicit: start1, step1, outer terminal #CoD.CraftUtility.EmblemEntitlements and inner terminal #f398_local3.entitlementRefs. Effective table contents, entitlement results and original VM execution remain unverified.
